- **cpo**: cpo role template (WaveX-authored, derived from session 2026-05-05/06 patterns) ([aimerdoux/wavex-os](https://github.com/aimerdoux/wavex-os/tree/HEAD/packages/agent-templates/cpo) · ★11)
- **cpo**: Lane: own the experiences catalog (the *what we sell*). ([aimerdoux/wavex-os](https://github.com/aimerdoux/wavex-os/tree/HEAD/packages/onboarding-ui/public/agent-templates/cpo) · ★11)
- **cpo**: The standing craft of a Chief Product Officer — every strategic question answered in four steps (diagnosis, at least two options with tradeoffs, a clear stance, next steps) under four golden rules. Load it as context when acting as CPO of a workspace; it is also activated by `role: cpo` in that workspace's node. ([pedroromeroluna/ai-first-product-skills](https://github.com/pedroromeroluna/ai-first-product-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cpo) · ★6)
- **cpo**: Chief Procurement Officer; sign-off authority for strategic POs and category-strategy approvals. ([arturcrmbot/zava-control-plane](https://github.com/arturcrmbot/zava-control-plane/tree/HEAD/api/server/personae/cpo) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **cpo**: Chief Product Officer - executive product strategy, organization design, decision system quality, and portfolio governance. Activate when: cpo, /cpo, "org design", "product org structure", "decision system", "portfolio governance", "PLT effectiveness", "executive product strategy" Do NOT activate for: PM-level requirements (product-manager), roadmap execution (director-product-management), GTM execution (director-product-marketing), financial modeling (bizops), individual feature work ([yohayetsion/product-org-os](https://github.com/yohayetsion/product-org-os/tree/HEAD/agents/cpo) · ★1)
- **cpo**: Compliance & Privacy Officer agent for the Virtual SaaS Team. Use this skill whenever the CPO agent needs to work: reviewing architecture for security risks, conducting a Data Protection Impact Assessment (DPIA) for any feature touching PII, maintaining the Security Controls Matrix, assessing regulatory compliance (GDPR, CCPA, SOC 2, HIPAA), reviewing third-party data sharing arrangements, responding to audit requests, or exercising a deployment veto. Also trigger for any task involving data classification, secrets management policy, penetration test scheduling, breach notification, or agent… ([elcoosp/elcoosp-skills](https://github.com/elcoosp/elcoosp-skills/tree/HEAD/virtual-saas-team/agents/core/cpo))
- **CPO**: Product scope, MVP boundaries, personas and prioritisation for Syntra KPI. ([harleyng/kpi-management](https://github.com/harleyng/kpi-management/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cpo))
- **cpo**: SDLC Chief Product Officer (CPO) owning product strategy, the roadmap, prioritization across the backlog, and requirements sign-off, and conducting the PM, Data Analyst, and Product Designer. Use for product direction, "what should we build next?", a roadmap or milestone plan, prioritizing a backlog, large ideation or refinement jobs, or product work needing more than one product role. Not for a single ticket (PM), an analysis (Data Analyst), or a UI spec (Product Designer). ([KnobanLabs/sdlc](https://github.com/KnobanLabs/sdlc/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/skills/cpo))
- **CPO / Chief Product Officer**: Drive product strategy with roadmap prioritization, team leadership, board communication, and competitive intelligence. ([clawic/skills](https://github.com/clawic/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cpo) · ★22 · 6 copies)
- **CPO Advisor**: Use when setting product vision, managing a product portfolio, measuring PMF, designing product teams, or prioritizing at portfolio level — strategic product leadership, not feature-level work. ([aashutosh396/mindpalace](https://github.com/aashutosh396/mindpalace/tree/HEAD/mindpalace/skills/global/product-management/cpo-advisor))
- **CPO Review — Roadmap Forcing Questions**: Use when committing a quarter's roadmap, deciding whether to kill a feature, or claiming PMF without a retention curve — runs six JTBD-driven forcing questions that cut the roadmap and surface what to ship vs kill. ([aashutosh396/mindpalace](https://github.com/aashutosh396/mindpalace/tree/HEAD/mindpalace/skills/global/product-management/cpo-review))
- **cpo-acceptance-criteria**: Writes the acceptance table for a change before it is built: each scenario with starting state, actor, steps, an observable passing result, how it is checked, plus the adversarial and missing-connector cases the quality executive verifies. Use when a roadmap item is approved, a bug is triaged as a fix, a connector, tool, template or language is about to be accepted, or someone asks "when is this done" or "what does done look like". ([melaya-labs/melaya-mcp](https://github.com/melaya-labs/melaya-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/excom-role-skills/cpo/cpo-acceptance-criteria) · ★1)
- **cpo-actor**: How the cpo-actor planning role is composed and operated. Consult when an operator-experience or product-sense judgment needs the org's accumulated wisdom (canon + prior decisions + relevant atoms) synthesized into a Plan atom for operator approval. Sister role to cto-actor; same primitive, different lens. ([stephengardner/layered-autonomous-governance](https://github.com/stephengardner/layered-autonomous-governance/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cpo-actor))
- **cpo-adoption-readout**: Reads whether a shipped feature is used and keeps people, with cohorts instead of totals: reach, activation, time to activate, repeat use and retention against a comparison cohort, ending in a keep, fix or retire call. Use when a release is two to six weeks old, in a monthly product review, before retiring a feature or template, or when someone asks "is anyone using this" or "did the launch work". ([melaya-labs/melaya-mcp](https://github.com/melaya-labs/melaya-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/excom-role-skills/cpo/cpo-adoption-readout) · ★1)
